      "code": "def build_graph(files: list[Path]) -> ProjectGraph:\n    \"\"\"Only the given files enter the graph, so edges to any other file are dropped.\"\"\"\n\n    modules = {file: read_module(file) for file in files}\n    units = [unit for file in files for unit in extract_units(file)]\n    resolver = Resolver(modules, units)\n    functions = [unit for unit in units if unit.kind == \"function\"]\n\n    # Repeated calls between the same two units keep only the first call site.\n\n    edges: dict[tuple[Unit, Unit], CallEdge] = {}\n    constructions: dict[tuple[Unit, str], Construction] = {}\n    for unit in functions:\n        for call in (node for node in ast.walk(unit.node) if isinstance(node, ast.Call)):\n            site = Location(unit.file, call.lineno, call.end_lineno or call.lineno)\n            edge, construction = resolver.resolve_call(unit, call, site)\n            if edge:\n                edges.setdefault((edge.caller, edge.callee), edge)\n            if construction:\n                constructions.setdefault((construction.unit, construction.class_name), construction)\n\n    return ProjectGraph(\n        units=units,\n        edges=list(edges.values()),\n        constructions=list(constructions.values()),\n        knowledge_pairs=knowledge_pairs(functions, modules),\n    )",

      "unit": "shotgun_surgery",
      "code": "def shotgun_surgery(duplicates: list[Duplicate], source: str) -> list[Finding]:\n    \"\"\"Groups of units joined by duplicated knowledge. The weakest link sets the probability.\"\"\"\n\n    neighbors: dict[Unit, set[Unit]] = defaultdict(set)\n    for duplicate in duplicates:\n        neighbors[duplicate.a].add(duplicate.b)\n        neighbors[duplicate.b].add(duplicate.a)\n\n    findings, seen = [], set()\n    for start in neighbors:\n        if start in seen:\n            continue\n        group = connected_units(start, neighbors)\n        seen |= group\n        if len(group) < MIN_SHOTGUN_UNITS:\n            continue\n\n        units = sorted(group, key=lambda unit: (str(unit.file), unit.start_line))\n        probability = min(d.probability for d in duplicates if d.a in group)\n        findings.append(\n            Finding(\n                location=units[0].location,\n                rule=SHOTGUN_SURGERY,\n                source=source,\n                message=f\"{', '.join(f'`{unit.name}`' for unit in units)}: {MESSAGES[SHOTGUN_SURGERY]}\",\n                probability=probability,\n                related=tuple(unit.location for unit in units[1:]),\n            )\n        )\n    return findings",
      "contract": {
        "inputs": [
          "duplicates: list[Duplicate]",

      "code": "def shotgun_surgery(duplicates: list[Duplicate], source: str) -> list[Finding]:\n    \"\"\"Groups of units joined by duplicated knowledge. The weakest link sets the probability.\"\"\"\n\n    neighbors: dict[Unit, set[Unit]] = defaultdict(set)\n    for duplicate in duplicates:\n        neighbors[duplicate.a].add(duplicate.b)\n        neighbors[duplicate.b].add(duplicate.a)\n\n    findings, seen = [], set()\n    for start in neighbors:\n        if start in seen:\n            continue\n        group = connected_units(start, neighbors)\n        seen |= group\n        if len(group) < MIN_SHOTGUN_UNITS:\n            continue\n\n        units = sorted(group, key=lambda unit: (str(unit.file), unit.start_line))\n        probability = min(d.probability for d in duplicates if d.a in group)\n        findings.append(\n            Finding(\n                location=units[0].location,\n                rule=SHOTGUN_SURGERY,\n                source=source,\n                message=f\"{', '.join(f'`{unit.name}`' for unit in units)}: {MESSAGES[SHOTGUN_SURGERY]}\",\n                probability=probability,\n                related=tuple(unit.location for unit in units[1:]),\n            )\n        )\n    return findings",

      "unit": "group_labels",
      "code": "def group_labels(keys):\n    \"\"\"Label each group by its state's filename and unit, or a hash when there is none or it collides.\"\"\"\n\n    def digest(body):\n        return hashlib.sha256(body.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]\n\n    def readable(state):\n        if not isinstance(state, dict) or not state.get(\"filename\"):\n            return None\n        stem = Path(state[\"filename\"]).stem\n        return f\"{stem}.{state['unit']}\" if state.get(\"unit\") else stem\n\n    labels = {}\n    for key in keys:\n        labels[key] = readable(json.loads(key[2])[\"state\"]) or digest(key[2])\n\n    counts = Counter((key[0], key[1], label) for key, label in labels.items())\n    for key, label in labels.items():\n        if counts[(key[0], key[1], label)] > 1:\n            labels[key] = f\"{label}_{digest(key[2])}\"\n    return labels",
